HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B006","root_000400/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_08578032bd11e5a94ca9","text":"2. **The earth discloses tested interior knowledge.** Fixed `ح د ث/B006` supplies disclosure or bringing out, and fixed `خ ب ر/B001` supplies both report and tested knowledge of an affair's interior rather than its outward appearance. This follows concealment being made to emerge (`خ ر ج/B002`) and precedes deeds being made visible (`ر ء ي/B012`). The two fixed words serve as the epistemic middle stage between extraction and display. The inference is that the earth has retained an interior record that its report now externalizes. `أَخْبَارَهَا` accordingly reads as evidentiary knowledge from within the earth, not generic news.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1613defc3088ac63b62f","text":"99:5 made the earth’s speech less autonomous and more commissioned: `تحدث` depends on `أوحى لها`. 99:7-8 also changed `أخبارها` from “news about the earth” toward “news about actions”: the reports are activated by the later visible `أعمال`, so the earth’s خبر becomes a moral archive.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002","root_000387/B002","root_000387/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3a43a724f0984b53a8c1","text":"4. **Soft-earth knowledge reading.** Lexical evidence: `خ ب ر B002` soft low ground and water-gathering place; `خ ب ر B003` working soft land through sharecropping; activated by `ء ر ض B002` fertile soft ground and `خ ر ج B003` yield from land. Structural cue: earth in 99:2 outputs burdens; earth in 99:4 reports. Assigned role: the earth is not only hard witness but worked ground whose inward softness retains traces and yields reports. Abductive move: I infer that cultivation/yield imagery can underwrite moral accounting: worked land gives back what was placed into it. Reading change: `أخبارها` reads as harvest-like output of a worked earth, not merely oral narration.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B002","root_000387/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5ce2a6ad86eda18225fb","text":"3. **The report can function as an agricultural ledger.** Fixed `خ ب ر/B002` supplies soft, low, water-gathering ground, and `B003` supplies cultivation for a known share of what the earth produces. Repeated `ٱلْأَرْضُ` activates the ground image; 99:6-8 then name intentional deeds (`ع م ل/B001`) and exact weight (`ث ق ل/B004`). The fixed `أَخْبَارَهَا` takes the role of a yield-account: the earth tells what each person's work produced. The move from human acts to planted or cultivated inputs is inferred, so this is exploratory. It makes the news a reckoning of produce rather than a detached verbal chronicle.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B002","root_000387/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_86ef5bd275469cfb653b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:4:3\",\"root_000387\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Haberler, insanların işlerinin yeryüzünde ürettiği payı kaydeden tarımsal bir hesap dökümü gibi de işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_88d2ab3a5c880135852e","text":"99:5 immediately corrected any reading of autonomous earth-speech by supplying its cause and authorization in `أَوْحَىٰ لَهَا`. Ayat 99:6-8 then revealed the report's practical output: deeds are displayed to dispersed people and resolved into singular, minute instances. That later movement activated fixed `ح د ث/B004` as a tale distributed to people and sharpened `خ ب ر/B001` from general news into evidence that culminates in sight.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B006","root_000400/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8d9bd55ce6a06dcc2abb","text":"2. **Disclosure after emergence.** Lexical evidence: `ح د ث B006` disclosure/bringing out; `خ ر ج B002` causing hidden matter to come out in 99:2. Structural cue: physical خروج precedes verbal تحديث. Assigned role: the ayah turns extracted matter into legible statement. Abductive move: I supply the two-step evidentiary process: first expose the contents, then disclose their meaning. Reading change: the ayah is not just “the earth tells news”; it is the interpretive phase after material evidence has been produced.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B006","root_000387/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ac08b0a625350217053c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:4:2\",\"root_000299\",[\"B006\"]],[\"99:4:3\",\"root_000387\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yerin haberi, yüzeydeki görüntüyü değil, içinde sınanıp saklanmış bilgiyi dışarı çıkaran kanıt niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B003","root_000387/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ae6463e291b5a0552e83","text":"1. **Earth’s report as newly arising speech.** Lexical evidence: `ح د ث B003` speech that arises as report/conversation; `خ ب ر B001` report and tested inner knowledge. Structural cue: the ayah answers the human’s `ما لها` by giving earth a reporting verb and an object of reports. Assigned role: `تحدث` is the act of converting inner knowledge into fresh report; `أخبارها` is the tested content. Abductive move: I infer that the earth reports from experience of what occurred upon/in it because `خ ب ر` includes inward tested knowledge, not mere transmitted rumor. Reading change: earth becomes a witness with experiential knowledge, not a passive stage.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B005","root_000638/B002","root_001272/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bb16b1a52f0dd0de294a","text":"3. **The calamity becomes narrated event.** Lexical evidence: `ح د ث B005` event/calamity of time; `ز ل ز ل B002` time’s shocks; `ق و ل B007` public circulating talk. Structural cue: `يومئذ` ties earth’s reporting to the decisive day opened by `إذا`. Assigned role: the quake is both event and the origin of reports about events. Abductive move: I infer that the earth’s reports include the history of events accumulated in time, because the packet links day, shocks, deeds, and seeing. Reading change: `أخبارها` expands from the earth’s present condition to the record of what time deposited in it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_df612d135a3c977ebdbd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:4:2\",\"root_000299\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Tek bir yer kaydı, ayrılmış her kişiye ulaşan ortak ve kişiselleştirilmiş bir anlatıya dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e0f0c6550f66389baa89","text":"4. **A private interior becomes a public and individualized tale.** Fixed `ح د ث/B004` supplies becoming a tale people tell. The earth speaks in the singular here, after human `ق و ل/B001`, but 99:6 disperses the people and 99:7-8 end with each person seeing the relevant act. The assigned role of `تُحَدِّثُ` is dissemination: one earth-record reaches separated recipients. The transmission from singular testimony to every individual is an inferred arrow. The report thus reads as the conversion of hidden local history into a universally distributed public record.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B001","root_000299/B003","root_000299/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e8064ac5cabb5be35862","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:4:2\",\"root_000299\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sarsıntı olayı, dışarıdan hakkında yorum yapılan bir şey olmaktan çıkıp kendi maddi oluşunu tanıklık olarak anlatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B001","root_000299/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e8f20f31c0f97b107d03","text":"1. **The event is converted into its own narration.** Fixed `ح د ث/B001` supplies an event coming into being, `B005` a calamity of time, and `B003` speech arising as report. The temporal `يَوْمَئِذٍ` points back to the quake and extraction, while the form `تُحَدِّثُ` turns that occurrence-root into an act of telling. The root's assigned role is to join happening and narration: what occurs becomes what is recounted. The claim that the material event itself encodes the report is inferred. Earth therefore does not merely comment on a calamity external to its speech; its event becomes its testimony.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
